loadDocument(Context context, Bundle arguments, @Nullable Document existingDocument) {\n        if (existingDocument != null) {\n            return existingDocument;\n        }\n\n        // When (filePath.getName().toLowerCase(Locale.getDefault()).endsWith(ext)) {\n                    document.setFileExtension(ext);\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n\n AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='## Summary\n\n**Root cause** — `DocumentIO.saveDocument()` decided whether to write the file by comparing the editor text against the *in-memory history\'s initial version*:\n\n```java\nDocument documentInitial = document.getInitialVersion();\nif (!currentText.equals(documentInitial.getContent())) { ...write... } else { ret = true; }  // silently no-op\n```\n\n`Document.getInitialVersion()` returns `_history.get(0)`, but **the editor history is disabled by default** (`AppSettings.isEditorHistoryEnabled()` → `false`), so `_history` stays empty and the "initial version" is only snapshotted lazily *inside* `saveDocument` — from the **already-modified** document. The snapshot therefore contains the current text, `currentText.equals(initial.getContent())` is `true`, and **nothing is ever written** while `saveDocument` reports success.\n\nThis matches the report exactly:\n- `DocumentEditFragment.onContentEditValueChanged()` copies the editor text into the `Document` only after a 5 s delay (`HISTORY_DELTA`) — the same moment the save icon becomes enabled.\n- Waiting for the icon ⇒ content already in the `Document` ⇒ the bogus snapshot ⇒ save skipped, and every later save (back press/`onPause`) skips too ⇒ **note never created**.\n- Pressing back immediately (steps 3–4 skipped) ⇒ content not yet in the `Document` ⇒ snapshot is empty ⇒ file written ⇒ works.\n\n**Fix** (`DocumentIO.saveDocument`): decide by comparing with what is *actually on disk*, and track the rename source via the document\'s previous file instead of the unreliable history entry. An untouched, never-stored document still creates no file, and unchanged files are still not rewritten.\n\nAlso hardened `DocumentEditFragment.saveDocument()` to point its arguments at the real file once stored, so the "new note in folder" arguments can no longer make `checkReloadDisk()` replace the saved note with a fresh empty document.\n\n**Verification** (Gradle can\'t run here — it fails at dependency download, identically before/after my change, since this environment has no network):\n- Compiled every source file with `javac` against the Android 27 SDK: both modified files compile cleanly, and the error set is byte-identical to the unpatched baseline (56 pre-existing errors, all in unrelated files due to missing third-party jars offline).\n- Ran the reported scenario end-to-end against the real compiled `DocumentIO`/`Document` classes: unpatched → note not created (4 failures incl. silent loss of edits to existing notes); patched → all pass, with both history settings.\n- Added `app/src/test/java/net/gsantner/markor/util/DocumentIoTest.java` (7 tests): fails 4/7 on the old code, passes 7/7 on the fixed code; the existing `DocumentTest` suite still passes.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 588, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 133306, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 588, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 8, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfYWDNjAoNgJ4SfW1SfBb', stop_reason=None, session_id='e8e40a80-43d3-4511-a27d-1798bcc5b40d', uuid='ea8a63b0-b69a-43f6-a196-ff53576b2d97')
